The exponent identity follows by induction:\nthe weighted sum of ell over ell=0,...,k-1 is2^k-k-1. Formula (5.1) involves\nno division by J or z and remains valid when either vanishes (0^0=1 only in\nthe empty-product cases). Embed the left Lambda^j with factor1 and\nthe right Lambda^j with factor k_j. For each nonterminal right query i add\n\n    Lambda_(i,p) += u_j h_i, Lambda_(i,z) += v_j h_i,\n    Lambda_(z,p) += w_j,    Lambda_(p,z) += z_j,\n    u_j=2lambda,\n    v_j=[2lambda*d_j+2(A_j+d_j)]/A_j,\n    k_j=2+lambda+(lambda+1)t_j,\n    w_j=4A_j+2d_j-2lambda(A_j-1), z_j=2A_j.           (3)\n\nThe last two additions occur once per node, not once per right query.\nAll unspecified contributions are zero and overlapping ones are summed.\nEquivalently, for every dyadic node of depth l+1 and offset o=k*2^(l+1),\nwrite its indicated four local original-index row contributions from (3).\nMultiply each by the product of k_r over strict ancestors whose right child\ncontains that node, and sum over nodes. This is a finite sum/product formula\nfor EVERY Lambda^j entry, without an optimizer or a computed recurrence.\n\nAll signs are positive: w_j=2lambda+2(d_j-A_j/lambda)>2lambda for j>=2;\nw_0=4+2a>0; w_1>2lambda-2 because A_1<lambda and d_1>0.\nThe other factors in (3) are positive. The identities\n\n    u_j+v_j=2(k_j-1),\n    u_j(A_j-1)+w_j-z_j=2(A_j+d_j)\n\nshow that divergence is -2h_i at every primitive nonterminal and 2S_j\nat its terminal. For example a right nonterminal has divergence\n-2k_j h_i+(u_j+v_j)h_i=-2h_i. At p the divergence is\n2S_j+z_j-u_j S_j-w_j=-2(1+d_j). Total divergence is zero,\nso the terminal has the asserted remaining divergence.\n\nFor the objective array start Kbase^0=0, mu^0_0=2. At a join put\n\n    s_j=(S_j+1+d_j)/(3S_j+2(1+d_j)), theta_j=1-s_j,\n    Dc=D_j, Dp=D_(j+1), p=n_j, R_i=2^j+i.\n\nHere Kbase is the finite array and mu is the outgoing optimum profile.\nEvery coefficient of its next depth is\n\n    Kbase_(l,v)=Lambda^j_lv/Dp                    (left indices),\n    Kbase_(R_i,R_v)=Kbase^j_iv                   (right indices),\n    Kbase_(R_i,p)=s_j mu^j_i                    (including i=n_j),\n    mu_l=2h_l/Dp                               (0<=l<n_j),\n    mu_p=2s_j-2S_j/Dp,\n    mu_(R_i)=theta_j mu^j_i.                         (4)\n\nEvery incoming-optimum multiplier is zero in this CHOSEN base. This is not\na claimed normal form for general certificates. All unlisted finite entries\nare zero. One can unroll (4) without any ambiguity: for an objective depth-r\nword, its right-spine node of depth l has offset o_l=2^r-2^l. Insert its\nleft primitive array Lambda^(l-1)/D_l on o_l,...,o_l+n_(l-1); its bridge\nis o_l+n_(l-1); every right query sends s_(l-1) times the right-child mu\nto that bridge. A query's mu is its local 2h_i/D_l if a left nonterminal,\nor 2s_(l-1)-2S_(l-1)/D_l if that bridge, times\nproduct_(t=l)^(r-1)theta_t. The terminal has mu_n=2product_(t=0)^(r-1)theta_t.\nThe unique first such left-or-bridge category assigns each nonterminal.\nTogether with the finite node sum for Lambda, these specify every original\nKbase and mu coefficient by finitely many sums/products of physical scalars.\n\nThe bridge sign is positive: writing A=S_j,Bjoin=1+d_j, the numerator is\n(A+Bjoin)Dp-A(3A+2Bjoin)=A^2+4ABjoin+2Bjoin^2+A+Bjoin>0.\nAlso 0<s_j,theta_j<1. Primitive flow pays the left mu; at p the left\nterminal divergence plus mu_p equals 2s_j, canceled by its incoming\nright mass 2s_j. At every right query the old mu is split into its new\ntheta_j mu and its new s_j mu edge. Hence original value cancellation is\n\n    sum_v Kbase_iv-sum_v Kbase_vi+mu_i=2*1_(i=n),\n    sum_i mu_i=2, mu_i>0.                             (5)\n\nNo row points into the ACTUAL terminal n in this base. Internal child\nterminal indices are still present, including their incoming rows.\n\n## 4. Full polynomial identities for the base\n\nNormalize L=1 and x_star=g_star=f_star=0, as in fact1252b8823bb5a290.\nAll original vectors (x_0,g_0,...,g_n) remain independent. The column Ppos\nhas entries Ppos_i below. Define\n\n    x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t, Ppos_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, y_i=x_i-g_i,\n    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,\n    V_j=2sum_(i<n_j)h_i(f_i-f_(n_j))\n          +sum_(i<n_j)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2\n          -A_j(A_j-1)||g_(n_j)||^2,\n    Psrc_j=V_j+sum_(i!=v)Lambda^j_iv Q_iv.\n\nFlow cancels every value in Psrc. Let r_R=sum_(right nonterminal i)h_i g_i,\nY=r_R-(A_j-1)g_z, Z=g_p-g_z. Expansion of the four unweighted row sums\nin (3), in their listed order, gives the gradient expressions\n\n    d_j<r_R,g_p>+||r_R||^2/2+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2/2\n                                          +(A_j-1)||g_p||^2/2;\n    -||r_R||^2/2+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2/2\n                        +(A_j-1)||g_z||^2/2-<r_R,g_z>;\n    d_j<g_z,g_p>+<g_z,r_R>+(||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2)/2;\n    -(d_j+1/2)||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2/2-<g_p,r_R>-<g_p,g_z>.\n\nFor example the sum of forward within-right displacement terms is\n-||r_R||^2/2+sum h_i^2||g_i||^2/2; this fixes its diagonal correction.\nMultiply the displays by u_j,v_j,w_j,z_j and add V and the child terms.\nUsing lambda^2=2lambda+1 gives the complete identity\n\n    Psrc_(j+1)=Psrc_j^left+k_j Psrc_j^right\n      +(tstar-t_j)[(lambda+1)||Y||^2-2lambda A_j<Y,Z>\n                         +A_j^2(2+tstar+t_j)||Z||^2].    (6)\n\nThis is an identity on all original gradients. In particular it makes no\npositive-epsilon primitive PSD assertion.\n\nLet Cbase^j be the entire gradient matrix of the objective polynomial after\n(5). Put T_R=sum_i mu^j_i g_(R_i), x_p=x_0-sum_(l<n_j)h_l g_l and\n\n    Rchild=Cbase^j(g_R)-Dc||T_R||^2/4,\n    kappa_j=Dc-Dp theta_j^2.\n\nExpansion of all (4) rows gives\n\n    Mbase_(j+1)(1/Dp)=Psrc_j(g_left)/Dp+Rchild\n     +||x_p+(S_j-Dp s_j)g_p-(Dp theta_j/2)T_R||^2/Dp\n     +(kappa_j/4)||T_R-2g_p||^2.                       (7)\n\nFor a coefficient check, after subtracting the first two terms the remaining\npolynomial is exactly\n\n    ||x_p||^2/Dp+2(S_j/Dp-s_j)<x_p,g_p>\n    +(S_j^2/Dp+2s_j)||g_p||^2-theta_j<x_p,T_R>\n    +(1+d_j-s_j)<g_p,T_R>+Dc||T_R||^2/4.\n\nExpanding the two squares agrees because\ns_j(3S_j+2(1+d_j))=S_j+1+d_j. Neither Rchild nor kappa_j is assumed\nnonnegative for the deformed hierarchy.\n\nAt the full Huber contact tuple x_0=D_j, g_i=1, every square argument\nand each Y,Z in (6) is zero. Induction from Mbase_0=||x_0-g_0||^2\nalso makes the derivative of Rchild zero at its common-gradient vector.\nThus, writing e=(1,...,1),\n\n    Cbase e=D mu/2, e^T Cbase e=D.                     (8)\n\nFor the actual m>=1536 (indeed m>=2), one useful original entry is\nCbase_00=2/D, with mu_0=2b/D: only the\nfirst primitive depth1 term (2-b^2)||g_0-g_1||^2 contributes to the first\ngradient diagonal in the left source, and the x_p square contributes b^2/D.\nThese statements are jointly derived here, not imported from the last report.\n\n## 5. The two explicit additions and every final sign\n\nThe physical non-leaf checkpoints are the odd indices p<n. If level(p)=l,\nthen 1<=l<=m-1 and p=o+2^l-1 for a unique cell offset\no=k*2^(l+1), 0<=k<2^(m-l-1). Define the complete cell of original\ngradient indices as o,...,o+2^(l+1)-1, and its short-leaf set\n\n    E_(l,o)={o,o+2,...,o+2^(l+1)-2}.\n\nThese are actual even queried indices; every one has h_i=b. No terminal\ngradient is a member of a leaf set or checkpoint set.\n\nLAW A, preceding leaf. For each non-leaf p of level l put\n\n    omega_p=epsilon*A_l^2/D,\n    J^A_(p-1,p)=J^A_(p,p-1)=omega_p.                  (9)\n\nLAW B, whole-cell fan. For each (l,o) and p=o+2^l-1 put\n\n    gamma_(l,o)=epsilon*A_l^2/(D*2^l),\n    J^B_(i,p) += gamma_(l,o), J^B_(p,i) += gamma_(l,o)\n                         for every i in E_(l,o).      (10)\n\nAll other entries of J are zero and all stated contributions are summed.\nThe two laws are different supports, not two fits of the old optimum-row\ntransport. The factor 2^l makes their total added mass identical at every\nscale, allowing a direct comparison without silently increasing a budget.\nFor either law the FINAL original coefficients are\n\n    K_ij=Kbase_ij+J_ij (i!=j), K_ii=0,\n    lambda_(i,star)=mu_i, lambda_(star,i)=0,\n    q=21/(20D).                                      (11)\n\nAll final multipliers are nonnegative. Symmetry J_ij=J_ji preserves every\nvalue-flow equation (5). The optimum profile is fixed as a deliberate\nrestriction of these two laws; no claim is made that a general slack-spending\nconstruction may be reduced to this restriction. No decrement is hidden.\n\nEach law has the ONE entire-word multiplier-mass account\n\n    T79=(epsilon/D)sum_(l=1)^(m-1)2^(m-l-1)A_l^2,\n    sum_(i!=j)J_ij=2T79.                             (12)\n\nThis is not a sufficient PSD budget. The actual global objective/contact\nbudget, including all cross terms between cells, is given next.\n\n## 6. Every full entry, the changed contact, and its ONE Schur budget\n\nFor ANY array of the form (11) define out_i=mu_i+sum_v K_iv. The entire\nmatrix C in\n\n    M(q)=q||x_0||^2-sum_i mu_i<x_0,g_i>\n                       +sum_(i,j)C_ij<g_i,g_j>\n\nhas entries\n\n    C_ii=out_i-1_(i=n)-h_i sum_(v>i)K_iv,\n    2C_ij=-(K_ij+K_ji)-h_j sum_(v>j)K_iv\n                         +h_i[mu_j+sum_(v<=i)K_jv], i<j. (13)\n\nThe complete original matrix therefore has initial entry q and initial\nmixed entries -mu_i/2. To derive (13), flow says half the incident row mass\nequals out_i-1_(i=n). A forward row sourced at i contributes -h_i K_iv\nto its diagonal. For i<j the difference squares give -(K_ij+K_ji),\nrows out of i ending after j give the second term, and rows out of j ending\nat/before i or at the optimum give the last term. This also accounts for\nevery physical intermediate gradient in a nonlocal pair.\n\nFor an unordered pair represented by u<v let L_uv(g)=sum_(t=u)^(v-1)h_t g_t\nand ell_uv=Ppos_v-Ppos_u. The exact added polynomial per unit weight is\n\n    Q_uv+Q_vu=||g_u-g_v||^2-<g_u-g_v,L_uv(g)>\n             =<g_u-g_v,y_v-y_u>.                     (14)\n\nFor v=u+1 across b this is\n(1-b)||g_u||^2+(b-2)<g_u,g_v>+||g_v||^2. Its determinant is -b^2/4,\nso it is indefinite whenever b>0. It is not a free PSD correction.\n\nAt g=e the value of (14) is zero, but its gradient is\nell_uv(e_v-e_u). Consequently define the entire contact-defect column\n\n    d79=(1/2)sum_(unordered {u,v},u<v)J_uv*ell_uv*(e_v-e_u).\n\nHere e_i denotes the standard coordinate column, distinct from e. Then\n\n    Ce=D mu/2+d79, e^T d79=0, e^T C e=D.              (15)\n\nFor A the formula is d79=(b/2)sum_p omega_p(e_p-e_(p-1)). For B it is\n(1/2)sum_(l,o)gamma_(l,o)sum_(i in E_(l,o))(Ppos_p-Ppos_i)(e_p-e_i),\nwith signed Ppos_p-Ppos_i valid on both sides of the checkpoint. Each is one\nfinite original-index vector, not separate contact credits. Both are nonzero:\n\n    Ppos^T d79=(1/2)sum_(u<v)J_uv(Ppos_v-Ppos_u)^2>0.           (16)\n\nAll physical steps are positive and each law has a positive added edge.\nThus Ce=D mu/2 is NOT assumed; it is false for both candidates.\n\nAt x_0=D,g=e the complete value is (qD-1)D=D/20. Its initial/common\ntwo-by-two block is\n\n    Bcommon=[[q,-1],[-1,D]], det Bcommon=qD-1=1/20.\n\nThis is the ONE scalar allowance for the whole word. Write arbitrary\ngradients as g=t e+v with e^T v=0, first in one real coordinate and then\ncoordinatewise in a Euclidean space. Set u=mu^T v and z=d79^T v. Their\nmixed column with (x_0,t) is (-u/2,Du/2+z). Exact inversion gives\n\n    (-u/2,Du/2+z) Bcommon^(-1) (-u/2,Du/2+z)^T\n       =D u^2/4+u z+[q/(qD-1)]z^2\n       =D u^2/4+u z+21z^2/D.                        (17)\n\nFor clarity the numerator before dividing by qD-1 is\nD(qD-1)u^2/4+(qD-1)uz+qz^2. Thus all contact, common-gradient and\ninitial costs have been charged exactly once. In particular the last term\nis the square of the WHOLE sum d79^T v and includes all cross-cell terms.\nIt cannot be paid anew from 1/20 at every node.\n\nEquivalently, the entire remaining gradient matrix is\n\n    R79=C-D mu mu^T/4-(mu d79^T+d79 mu^T)/2\n                                      -(21/D)d79 d79^T,\n    R79 e=0.                                        (18)\n\nFull PSD at q=21/(20D) is equivalent to R79 being PSD on the entire\ne-perpendicular space, or equivalently on all gradient coordinates since\nR79 e=0. Equations (17)--(18) are a necessary-and-sufficient expenditure\nidentity, NOT a claim that either law pays it successfully. The direct\nnegative full-polynomial test below disproves that claim.\n\n## 7. Actual terminal-square expenditure\n\nNeither added support touches n. The final incoming-terminal coefficients\nremain zero by the explicit formulas, not by deleting rows from a supplied\ncertificate. Full terminal flow is therefore sum_j lambda_(n,j)=2 and\nC_nn=1. More explicitly the receivers are star and the right-spine bridges\n\n    p_l=2^m-2^l-1, 0<=l<m,\n    alpha_star=product_(v=0)^(m-1)theta_v,\n    alpha_(p_l)=s_l product_(v=0)^(l-1)theta_v.        (19)\n\nAn empty product is1. All other alpha_i are zero. The finite telescoping\nidentity sum_l s_l product_(v<l)theta_v=1-product_(v<m)theta_v shows these\nare probabilities. They are exactly lambda_(n,j)/2 from (4), including\nthe adjacent final bridge p_0=n-1. No unqueried gradient is discarded.\n\nThe exact vector in the terminal square is\n\n    Vterm=alpha_star x_0\n         -sum_(t<n)[h_t w_t-(w_t-w_(t-1))]g_t,\n    w_(-1)=alpha_star, w_t=alpha_star+sum_(i=0)^t alpha_i.\n\nThe complete remainder is\n\n    M(q)=||g_n-Vterm||^2+Rprefix,\n    Rprefix=q||x_0||^2-2sum_(j in {star,0,...,n-1})alpha_j\n                    (f_j-||g_j||^2/2)-||Vterm||^2\n                  +sum_(i!=j in {star,0,...,n-1})lambda_ij Q_ij. (20)\n\nThe prefix flow cancels every f_j. Formula (20) follows by expanding the\nterminal rows, as in\nfact1252b8823bb5a290(c), and retains the actual negative square cost.\nEquivalently put vterm=(mu_n/2)x_0-sum_(i<n)C_in g_i; then Vterm=vterm\nand Rprefix=q||x_0||^2-sum_(i<n)mu_i<x_0,g_i>\n+sum_(i,j<n)C_ij<g_i,g_j>-||vterm||^2.\nSince the added rows contain no terminal gradients, they do not alter\nC_in or vterm. They add their FULL (14) expressions to Rprefix.\n\n## 8. Original-space block direction and its complete base action\n\nPut\n\n    W79=A_2+d_2, Bfirst=b+1+d_1,\n    Edef=(t_2-tstar)A_2^2(lambda+t_2)>0,\n    delta3=t_3-tstar>0.\n\nW79 is the physical mass of steps0,...,3 and of steps8,...,11.\nThe notation is local and does not replace W_n(h). In the ENTIRE original\nformal space assign scalar values\n\n    x_0=0,\n    g_i=1 for i=0,1,2,3;\n    g_i=-1 for i=8,9,10,11;\n    g_i=0 for every other i=0,...,n.                 (21)\n\nThe two active four-step blocks have identical physical words. Hence the\ntotal displacement is W79-W79=0. All positions from index12 onward are0;\ng_7=g_15=g_n=0. These are chosen test values, not equality constraints\non formal coordinates and not a legal primal trajectory.\n\nIn the first H3, its left H2 gradients are all1, its right H2 all0,\nso both child Psrc terms vanish by (6), and Y=0,Z=1 gives -Edef.\nThe H3 beginning at8 gives the same -Edef. At their H4 join,\nY=-W79,Z=0. Thus the COMPLETE source polynomial is\n\n    Psrc_4=-(1+k_3)Edef-delta3(lambda+1)W79^2<0.     (22)\n\nFor every containing source ancestor all its right gradients, bridge and\nterminal are zero, so Y=Z=0 and (6) propagates exactly (22), with left\nfactor1. At the top objective join the left displacement, x_p, g_p and\nT_R are allzero; Rchild on its zero right gradients is zero. Equation (7)\ntherefore gives for every m>=5, in particular all m>=1536,\n\n    Mbase(q;21)=Psrc_4/D<0 for EVERY real q.           (23)\n\nNo positive-epsilon primitive child or quotient feasibility has been used.\nThe parameter signs came from the exact SAME-m (2).\n\n## 9. Law A: every preceding-leaf symmetric pair vanishes\n\nEvery edge in (9) connects an even index to the next odd index. Assignment\n(21) is constant on every such pair, including pairs outside its support.\nThus g_(p-1)-g_p=0 and its ENTIRE (14) polynomial is zero. All preceding\ngradient costs and cross terms cancel exactly; no diagonal-only inference\nis involved. Summing every added pair gives\n\n    M_A(q;21)=Mbase(q;21)=Psrc_4/D<0.                 (24)\n\nThis proves a stronger, precise scope: for the fixed inherited base, ANY\nnonnegative weights omega_p on this support fail for every q. Allowing\ndifferent scale laws, horizon dependence, arbitrary magnitudes or zero\nweights cannot change (24). The new support is not a repair of the earlier\nH4 defect. This is a formal PSD obstruction, not an interpolable witness.\n\n## 10. Law B: every full-cell fan has nonpositive action\n\nFor a cell define its UNWEIGHTED full polynomial\n\n    F_(l,o)=sum_(i in E_(l,o))<g_i-g_p,y_p-y_i>, p=o+2^l-1.\n\nThen M_B-Mbase=sum_(l,o)gamma_(l,o)F_(l,o), with no omitted rows.\nWe evaluate every nonzero cell on (21), including cells whose checkpoint\nis zero but whose source leaves are nonzero.\n\nAt l=1 each cell comprises four consecutive gradient indices; (21) is\nconstant on that cell. Its fan is zero. At l=2 only the cells o=0 and\no=8 can contribute. For o=0, p=3 has gradient1. The left leaves0,2 have\nthe same gradient and contribute zero. The right leaves4,6 have gradient0,\nposition -W79 and y=-W79, whereas y_3=-A_2. Each contributes\n(-1)(W79-A_2)=-d_2. At o=8 the entire geometry through15 is the negative\nof the first cell, apart from a common positional translation. A common\ntranslation cancels in y_p-y_i, and negating both factors preserves their\nproduct. Its two nonzero terms likewise equal -d_2. Therefore\n\n    F_(2,0)=F_(2,8)=-2d_2<0.                        (25)\n\nAt l=3 only the first cell is active, with p=7, g_p=0 and y_p=-W79.\nIts nonzero source leaves are0,2,8,10. Their original proximal positions\nare respectively -1,-Bfirst-1,-W79+1,-W79+Bfirst+1.\nThe four complete terms, in this order, are\n\n    1-W79, 1-W79+Bfirst, 1, 1+Bfirst.\n\nTheir sum is 4-2W79+2Bfirst. Since\nW79=2b+2+d_1+d_2 and Bfirst=b+1+d_1, this is\n\n    F_(3,0)=-2(b+d_2-1)<0.                          (26)\n\nThe positive terms1 and1+Bfirst have been explicitly paid by the negative\nterms; they are not omitted. At EVERY l>=4 only the first cell can have\nnonzero endpoint gradients, and its checkpoint has y_p=g_p=0. The same\nfour source leaves now contribute\n\n    1, 1+Bfirst, 1-W79, 1-W79+Bfirst,\n\nwhose sum is again -2(b+d_2-1). Every other source and checkpoint has\nzero gradient, so its factor g_i-g_p is zero, even if a position is nonzero.\nAll cells not listed are either disjoint from the support or were covered\nby the constant-cell calculation. Hence the ENTIRE added value is\n\n    M_B(q;21)-Mbase(q;21)\n      =-2d_2[gamma_(2,0)+gamma_(2,8)]\n       -2(b+d_2-1)sum_(l=3)^(m-1)gamma_(l,0)<0.      (27)\n\nThis proves M_B(q;21)<0 at every real q. More generally (27) is nonpositive\nfor arbitrary nonnegative weights gamma_(l,o), provided each individual\ncell uses the SAME weight for all of its even source leaves. Thus every\nnonnegative combination of these uniform full-cell fans, and also every\naddition of the adjacent pairs from Section9, fails on the fixed base.\nThis is a consequence of the same two support calculations, not a third\nlaw or extra search. Arbitrary unequal within-cell weights are NOT covered.\n\n## 11. Boundary calibrations and scope audit\n\nFor n=0, the empty base uses mu_0=2 and M(q)=||x_0-g_0||^2+(q-1)||x_0||^2.\nThere are no additions in (9)--(10). For an arbitrary all-zero word, the\nseparate accepted calibration is lambda_(n,star)=2 with all other rowszero,\ngiving M(1)=||x_0-g_n||^2 on every original independent vector, including\nduplicate-position gradients. It is not obtained by substituting a fictitious\npositive hierarchy into the zero word. The general zero-step embedding is\nexactly that of fact1252b8823bb5a290; neither new law asserts an arbitrary-word\nextension or removes any formal coordinate at a zero step.\n\nFor a one-step hierarchy there is no non-leaf checkpoint. Direct expansion\nof (3)--(7) gives\n\n    Psrc_1=(2-b^2)||g_0-g_1||^2,\n    Mbase_1(1/D_1)=||x_0-D_1(g_0+g_1)/2||^2/D_1\n                              +(3-2b)||g_0-g_1||^2/4.\n\nThis is full PSD because b<sqrt(2)<3/2. It does not prove later PSD.\n\nFor the epsilon=0 Silver calibration, retain the same definitions with\nepsilon=0, so A_j=lambda^j,d_j=A_j/lambda and t_j=tstar. Both additions\nvanish identically; (6) gives Psrc_j=0 at all depths. Write A=S_j and\nBjoin=1+tstar(A+1). Direct expansion of kappa_j in (7) gives\n\n    (3A+2Bjoin)^2 kappa_j\n      =2(1-tstar)A^3+(6-8tstar)A^2\n        +(1+tstar)(8tstar-2)A+(1+tstar)^2(1-2tstar)>0.\n\nAll coefficients are positive since 1/4<tstar<1/2. Starting at the empty\nsquare, the Schur remainder Rchild of a PSD child with positive initial\ncoefficient is PSD, and (7) proves full Silver PSD inductively. This invokes\nchild PSD ONLY at epsilon=0, where it has just been established. The original\nq=21/(20D) merely adds ||x_0||^2/(20D). It does not change the failures for\npositive epsilon. No Silver calibration is imported to a deformed child.\n\nThe topology note021361fbf2347def explicitly asked for comparison with earlier\nfinite-row repairs. It does not cover a changed base, signed reallocations,\nnonuniform within-cell fan weights, or the unrestricted full dual.\n\n## 1. Original target and exact accepted scopes\n\nFor every integer n>=0 and word h in [0,infinity)^n, W_n(h) is the supremum\nof 2(f(x_n)-f(x_star))/(L||x_0-x_star||^2) over all finite-dimensional real\nEuclidean spaces, convex differentiable L-smooth functions with an attained\nminimizer x_star, and x_0!=x_star. Thus W=2R.\n\nThe ORIGINAL benchmark is C_0=B_0=1 and\n\n    Omega(u,v)=(u+v+sqrt((u+v)^2+4uv))/2,\n    G(u,v)=2u+v/2+sqrt(v^2+8uv)/2,\n    C_n=max_(0<=k<n)Omega(C_k,C_(n-k-1)),\n    B_n=max_(0<=k<n)G(C_k,B_(n-k-1)), U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.\n\nAll splits are integers. The target asks whether\nlim_n B_n inf_(h in [0,infinity)^n)W_n(h)=1; an affirmative lower statement\nrequires, for every eta>0, a word-independent N_eta such that every actual\nn>=N_eta and every such word has B_n W_n(h)>=1-eta.\n\nAccepted fact1252b8823bb5a290 supplies the original interpolation convention,\nthe full independent-vector certificate, and exact terminal completion.\nAccepted fact04219554e41d9750 supplies its stated primitive/objective\ncomposition identities and benchmark, not an unconditional primitive seed.\nOnly the benchmark conclusion of fact5fd396eba75651e7, Section10, is used:\n\n    B_(2^m-1)/D_m<237/250 for every m>=1536, tau=1/4.       (1)\n\nHere D_m and the SAME-enclosing-m word are defined in Section2. A full-class\nbound W_(2^m-1)(H_m)<=21/(20D_m) would therefore imply\nB_n W_n(H_m)<4977/5000 on unbounded ACTUAL horizons. This draft does not\nobtain that bound. A failed certificate supplies no upper or lower bound on W.\n\n## 2. Fixed physical hierarchy\n\nFix m>=1536 throughout and set\n\n    lambda=1+sqrt(2), epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon,\n    c=lambda(1+epsilon), a=beta/c, tstar=1/lambda,\n    Hword_0=(), Hword_(j+1)=(Hword_j,1+d_j,Hword_j), d_j=a*c^j,\n    n_j=2^j-1, A_j=1+sum Hword_j, S_j=A_j-1,\n    D_j=2A_j-1, t_j=d_j/A_j, h=H_m=Hword_m, n=n_m, D=D_m.\n\nEvery child retains this enclosing epsilon. In original indices,\nh_i=1+a*c^level(i), where level(i) is the largest integer l with\n2^l dividing i+1, for 0<=i<n. In particular every even query has\nphysical step b=1+a. The index n is the unqueried terminal; set h_n=0\nonly as notation in matrix formulas. No terminal step is executed.\n\nThe exact scalar identities, which are not executed recurrences, are\n\n    A_j=v_h*c^j+(1-v_h)*2^j, v_h=a/(c-2),\n    A_(j+1)=(2+t_j)A_j, t_(j+1)=c*t_j/(2+t_j).\n\nThey follow by summing the physical concatenation and solving its geometric\nsum. Since c(c-2)>1>beta, one has 0<v_h<1. Consequently t_j increases\nstrictly and 0<t_j<c-2<1/2. For the final inequality,\nlambda<17/7 and epsilon<=1/6144 imply c<5/2.\nDirect substitution gives\n\n    b=sqrt(2)-2epsilon/[lambda(1+epsilon)],\n    A_1=lambda-2epsilon/[lambda(1+epsilon)], d_1=1-epsilon,\n    A_2=lambda^2-epsilon[1+4/(lambda(1+epsilon))],\n    d_2=lambda(1-epsilon^2),\n    lambda*d_2-A_2\n      =epsilon[1+4/(lambda(1+epsilon))-lambda^2 epsilon]>0. (2)\n\nThe bracket is positive since lambda^2<6 and epsilon<=1/6144.\nAlso lambda*d_1-A_1=epsilon[-lambda+2/(lambda(1+epsilon))]<0.\nThus t_0,t_1<tstar<t_2<=t_j for j>=2. In particular b>1,d_2>0,\nand t_3>tstar. These strict signs are all that the negative test needs.\n\n## 3. Every inherited primitive and objective coefficient\n\nThe inherited array is rederived here. Its prior unverified reports are\nawareness, not premises. A primitive depth-j array Lambda^j has indices\n0,...,n_j. Start with Lambda^0=0 and zero diagonal. At a depth-(j+1)\nnode, put p=n_j, z=n_(j+1). Embed the left Lambda^j with factor1 and\nthe right Lambda^j with factor k_j. For each nonterminal right query i add\n\n    Lambda_(i,p) += u_j h_i, Lambda_(i,z) += v_j h_i,\n    Lambda_(z,p) += w_j,    Lambda_(p,z) += z_j,\n    u_j=2lambda,\n    v_j=[2lambda*d_j+2(A_j+d_j)]/A_j,\n    k_j=2+lambda+(lambda+1)t_j,\n    w_j=4A_j+2d_j-2lambda(A_j-1), z_j=2A_j.           (3)\n\nThe last two additions occur once per node, not once per right query.\nAll unspecified contributions are zero and overlapping ones are summed.\nEquivalently, for every dyadic node of depth l+1 and offset o=k*2^(l+1),\nwrite its indicated four local original-index row contributions from (3).\nMultiply each by the product of k_r over strict ancestors whose right child\ncontains that node, and sum over nodes. This is a finite sum/product formula\nfor EVERY Lambda^j entry, without an optimizer or a computed recurrence.\n\nAll signs are positive: w_j=2lambda+2(d_j-A_j/lambda)>2lambda for j>=2;\nw_0=4+2a>0; w_1>2lambda-2 because A_1<lambda and d_1>0.\nThe other factors in (3) are positive. The identities\n\n    u_j+v_j=2(k_j-1),\n    u_j(A_j-1)+w_j-z_j=2(A_j+d_j)\n\nshow that divergence is -2h_i at every primitive nonterminal and 2S_j\nat its terminal. For example a right nonterminal has divergence\n-2k_j h_i+(u_j+v_j)h_i=-2h_i. At p the divergence is\n2S_j+z_j-u_j S_j-w_j=-2(1+d_j). Total divergence is zero,\nso the terminal has the asserted remaining divergence.\n\nFor the objective array start Kbase^0=0, mu^0_0=2. At a join put\n\n    s_j=(S_j+1+d_j)/(3S_j+2(1+d_j)), theta_j=1-s_j,\n    Dc=D_j, Dp=D_(j+1), p=n_j, R_i=2^j+i.\n\nHere Kbase is the finite array and mu is the outgoing optimum profile.\nEvery coefficient of its next depth is\n\n    Kbase_(l,v)=Lambda^j_lv/Dp                    (left indices),\n    Kbase_(R_i,R_v)=Kbase^j_iv                   (right indices),\n    Kbase_(R_i,p)=s_j mu^j_i                    (including i=n_j),\n    mu_l=2h_l/Dp                               (0<=l<n_j),\n    mu_p=2s_j-2S_j/Dp,\n    mu_(R_i)=theta_j mu^j_i.                         (4)\n\nEvery incoming-optimum multiplier is zero in this CHOSEN base. This is not\na claimed normal form for general certificates. All unlisted finite entries\nare zero. One can unroll (4) without any ambiguity: for an objective depth-r\nword, its right-spine node of depth l has offset o_l=2^r-2^l. Insert its\nleft primitive array Lambda^(l-1)/D_l on o_l,...,o_l+n_(l-1); its bridge\nis o_l+n_(l-1); every right query sends s_(l-1) times the right-child mu\nto that bridge. A query's mu is its local 2h_i/D_l if a left nonterminal,\nor 2s_(l-1)-2S_(l-1)/D_l if that bridge, times\nproduct_(t=l)^(r-1)theta_t. The terminal has mu_n=2product_(t=0)^(r-1)theta_t.\nThe unique first such left-or-bridge category assigns each nonterminal.\nTogether with the finite node sum for Lambda, these specify every original\nKbase and mu coefficient by finitely many sums/products of physical scalars.\n\nThe bridge sign is positive: writing A=S_j,Bjoin=1+d_j, the numerator is\n(A+Bjoin)Dp-A(3A+2Bjoin)=A^2+4ABjoin+2Bjoin^2+A+Bjoin>0.\nAlso 0<s_j,theta_j<1. Primitive flow pays the left mu; at p the left\nterminal divergence plus mu_p equals 2s_j, canceled by its incoming\nright mass 2s_j. At every right query the old mu is split into its new\ntheta_j mu and its new s_j mu edge. Hence original value cancellation is\n\n    sum_v Kbase_iv-sum_v Kbase_vi+mu_i=2*1_(i=n),\n    sum_i mu_i=2, mu_i>0.                             (5)\n\nNo row points into the ACTUAL terminal n in this base. Internal child\nterminal indices are still present, including their incoming rows.\n\n## 4. Full polynomial identities for the base\n\nNormalize L=1 and x_star=g_star=f_star=0, as in fact1252b8823bb5a290.\nAll original vectors (x_0,g_0,...,g_n) remain independent. The column Ppos\nhas entries Ppos_i below. Define\n\n    x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t, Ppos_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, y_i=x_i-g_i,\n    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,\n    V_j=2sum_(i<n_j)h_i(f_i-f_(n_j))\n          +sum_(i<n_j)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2\n          -A_j(A_j-1)||g_(n_j)||^2,\n    Psrc_j=V_j+sum_(i!=v)Lambda^j_iv Q_iv.\n\nFlow cancels every value in Psrc. Define the complete cell of original\ngradient indices as o,...,o+2^(l+1)-1, and its short-leaf set\n\n    E_(l,o)={o,o+2,...,o+2^(l+1)-2}.\n\nThese are actual even queried indices; every one has h_i=b. (12)\n\nThis is not a sufficient PSD budget. The actual global objective/contact\nbudget, including all cross terms between cells, is given next.\n\n## 6. Every full entry, the changed contact, and its ONE Schur budget\n\nFor ANY array of the form (11) define out_i=mu_i+sum_v K_iv. The entire\nmatrix C in\n\n    M(q)=q||x_0||^2-sum_i mu_i<x_0,g_i>\n                       +sum_(i,j)C_ij<g_i,g_j>\n\nhas entries\n\n    C_ii=out_i-1_(i=n)-h_i sum_(v>i)K_iv,\n    2C_ij=-(K_ij+K_ji)-h_j sum_(v>j)K_iv\n                         +h_i[mu_j+sum_(v<=i)K_jv], i<j. (13)\n\nThe complete original matrix therefore has initial entry q and initial\nmixed entries -mu_i/2. To derive (13), flow says half the incident row mass\nequals out_i-1_(i=n). A forward row sourced at i contributes -h_i K_iv\nto its diagonal. For i<j the difference squares give -(K_ij+K_ji),\nrows out of i ending after j give the second term, and rows out of j ending\nat/before i or at the optimum give the last term. This also accounts for\nevery physical intermediate gradient in a nonlocal pair.\n\nFor an unordered pair represented by u<v let L_uv(g)=sum_(t=u)^(v-1)h_t g_t\nand ell_uv=Ppos_v-Ppos_u. The exact added polynomial per unit weight is\n\n    Q_uv+Q_vu=||g_u-g_v||^2-<g_u-g_v,L_uv(g)>\n             =<g_u-g_v,y_v-y_u>.                     (14)\n\nFor v=u+1 across b this is\n(1-b)||g_u||^2+(b-2)<g_u,g_v>+||g_v||^2. Its determinant is -b^2/4,\nso it is indefinite whenever b>0. It is not a free PSD correction.\n\nAt g=e the value of (14) is zero, but its gradient is\nell_uv(e_v-e_u). Consequently define the entire contact-defect column\n\n    d79=(1/2)sum_(unordered {u,v},u<v)J_uv*ell_uv*(e_v-e_u).\n\nHere e_i denotes the standard coordinate column, distinct from e. Then\n\n    Ce=D mu/2+d79, e^T d79=0, e^T C e=D.              (15)\n\nFor A the formula is d79=(b/2)sum_p omega_p(e_p-e_(p-1)). For B it is\n(1/2)sum_(l,o)gamma_(l,o)sum_(i in E_(l,o))(Ppos_p-Ppos_i)(e_p-e_i),\nwith signed Ppos_p-Ppos_i valid on both sides of the checkpoint. Each is one\nfinite original-index vector, not separate contact credits. Both are nonzero:\n\n    Ppos^T d79=(1/2)sum_(u<v)J_uv(Ppos_v-Ppos_u)^2>0.           (16)\n\nAll physical steps are positive and each law has a positive added edge.\nThus Ce=D mu/2 is NOT assumed; it is false for both candidates.\n\nAt x_0=D,g=e the complete value is (qD-1)D=D/20. Its initial/common\ntwo-by-two block is\n\n    Bcommon=[[q,-1],[-1,D]], det Bcommon=qD-1=1/20.\n\nThis is the ONE scalar allowance for the whole word. Write arbitrary\ngradients as g=t e+v with e^T v=0, first in one real coordinate and then\ncoordinatewise in a Euclidean space. Set u=mu^T v and z=d79^T v. Their\nmixed column with (x_0,t) is (-u/2,Du/2+z). Exact inversion gives\n\n    (-u/2,Du/2+z) Bcommon^(-1) (-u/2,Du/2+z)^T\n       =D u^2/4+u z+[q/(qD-1)]z^2\n       =D u^2/4+u z+21z^2/D.                        (17)\n\nFor clarity the numerator before dividing by qD-1 is\nD(qD-1)u^2/4+(qD-1)uz+qz^2. Thus all contact, common-gradient and\ninitial costs have been charged exactly once. In particular the last term\nis the square of the WHOLE sum d79^T v and includes all cross-cell terms.\nIt cannot be paid anew from 1/20 at every node.\n\nEquivalently, the entire remaining gradient matrix is\n\n    R79=C-D mu mu^T/4-(mu d79^T+d79 mu^T)/2\n                                      -(21/D)d79 d79^T,\n    R79 e=0.                                        (18)\n\nFull PSD at q=21/(20D) is equivalent to R79 being PSD on the entire\ne-perpendicular space, or equivalently on all gradient coordinates since\nR79 e=0. Equations (17)--(18) are a necessary-and-sufficient expenditure\nidentity, NOT a claim that either law pays it successfully. The direct\nnegative full-polynomial test below disproves that claim.\n\n## 7. Actual terminal-square expenditure\n\nNeither added support touches n. The final incoming-terminal coefficients\nremain zero by the explicit formulas, not by deleting rows from a supplied\ncertificate. Full terminal flow is therefore sum_j lambda_(n,j)=2 and\nC_nn=1. More explicitly the receivers are star and the right-spine bridges\n\n    p_l=2^m-2^l-1, 0<=l<m,\n    alpha_star=product_(v=0)^(m-1)theta_v,\n    alpha_(p_l)=s_l product_(v=0)^(l-1)theta_v.        (19)\n\nAn empty product is1. All other alpha_i are zero. The finite telescoping\nidentity sum_l s_l product_(v<l)theta_v=1-product_(v<m)theta_v shows these\nare probabilities. They are exactly lambda_(n,j)/2 from (4), including\nthe adjacent final bridge p_0=n-1. No unqueried gradient is discarded.\n\nThe exact vector in the terminal square is\n\n    Vterm=alpha_star x_0\n         -sum_(t<n)[h_t w_t-(w_t-w_(t-1))]g_t,\n    w_(-1)=alpha_star, w_t=alpha_star+sum_(i=0)^t alpha_i.\n\nThe complete remainder is\n\n    M(q)=||g_n-Vterm||^2+Rprefix,\n    Rprefix=q||x_0||^2-2sum_(j in {star,0,...,n-1})alpha_j\n                    (f_j-||g_j||^2/2)-||Vterm||^2\n                  +sum_(i!=j in {star,0,...,n-1})lambda_ij Q_ij. (20)\n\nThe prefix flow cancels every f_j. Formula (20) follows by expanding the\nterminal rows, as in\nfact1252b8823bb5a290(c), and retains the actual negative square cost.\nEquivalently put vterm=(mu_n/2)x_0-sum_(i<n)C_in g_i; then Vterm=vterm\nand Rprefix=q||x_0||^2-sum_(i<n)mu_i<x_0,g_i>\n+sum_(i,j<n)C_ij<g_i,g_j>-||vterm||^2.\nSince the added rows contain no terminal gradients, they do not alter\nC_in or vterm. They add their FULL (14) expressions to Rprefix.\n\n## 8. Original-space block direction and its complete base action\n\nPut\n\n    W79=A_2+d_2, Bfirst=b+1+d_1,\n    Edef=(t_2-tstar)A_2^2(lambda+t_2)>0,\n    delta3=t_3-tstar>0.\n\nW79 is the physical mass of steps0,...,3 and of steps8,...,11.\nThe notation is local and does not replace W_n(h). In the ENTIRE original\nformal space assign scalar values\n\n    x_0=0,\n    g_i=1 for i=0,1,2,3;\n    g_i=-1 for i=8,9,10,11;\n    g_i=0 for every other i=0,...,n. These are chosen test values, not equality constraints\non formal coordinates and not a legal primal trajectory.\n\nIn the first H3, its left H2 gradients are all1, its right H2 all0,\nso both child Psrc terms vanish by (6), and Y=0,Z=1 gives -Edef.\nThe H3 beginning at8 gives the same -Edef. At the top objective join the left displacement, x_p, g_p and\nT_R are allzero; Rchild on its zero right gradients is zero. Equation (7)\ntherefore gives for every m>=5, in particular all m>=1536,\n\n    Mbase(q;21)=Psrc_4/D<0 for EVERY real q.           (23)\n\nNo positive-epsilon primitive child or quotient feasibility has been used.\nThe parameter signs came from the exact SAME-m (2).\n\n## 9. Law A: every preceding-leaf symmetric pair vanishes\n\nEvery edge in (9) connects an even index to the next odd index. All preceding\ngradient costs and cross terms cancel exactly; no diagonal-only inference\nis involved. Summing every added pair gives\n\n    M_A(q;21)=Mbase(q;21)=Psrc_4/D<0.                 (24)\n\nThis proves a stronger, precise scope: for the fixed inherited base, ANY\nnonnegative weights omega_p on this support fail for every q. (1)\n\nHere D_m and the SAME-enclosing-m word are defined in Section2. A full-class\nbound W_(2^m-1)(H_m)<=21/(20D_m) would therefore imply\nB_n W_n(H_m)<4977/5000 on unbounded ACTUAL horizons. This draft does not\nobtain that bound. A failed certificate supplies no upper or lower bound on W.\n\n## 2. Fixed physical hierarchy\n\nFix m>=1536 throughout and set\n\n    lambda=1+sqrt(2), epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon,\n    c=lambda(1+epsilon), a=beta/c, tstar=1/lambda,\n    Hword_0=(), Hword_(j+1)=(Hword_j,1+d_j,Hword_j), d_j=a*c^j,\n    n_j=2^j-1, A_j=1+sum Hword_j, S_j=A_j-1,\n    D_j=2A_j-1, t_j=d_j/A_j, h=H_m=Hword_m, n=n_m, D=D_m.\n\nEvery child retains this enclosing epsilon. In original indices,\nh_i=1+a*c^level(i), where level(i) is the largest integer l with\n2^l dividing i+1, for 0<=i<n. In particular every even query has\nphysical step b=1+a. The index n is the unqueried terminal; set h_n=0\nonly as notation in matrix formulas. No terminal step is executed.\n\nThe exact scalar identities, which are not executed recurrences, are\n\n    A_j=v_h*c^j+(1-v_h)*2^j, v_h=a/(c-2),\n    A_(j+1)=(2+t_j)A_j, t_(j+1)=c*t_j/(2+t_j).\n\nThey follow by summing the physical concatenation and solving its geometric\nsum. Since c(c-2)>1>beta, one has 0<v_h<1. Consequently t_j increases\nstrictly and 0<t_j<c-2<1/2. For the final inequality,\nlambda<17/7 and epsilon<=1/6144 imply c<5/2.\nDirect substitution gives\n\n    b=sqrt(2)-2epsilon/[lambda(1+epsilon)],\n    A_1=lambda-2epsilon/[lambda(1+epsilon)], d_1=1-epsilon,\n    A_2=lambda^2-epsilon[1+4/(lambda(1+epsilon))],\n    d_2=lambda(1-epsilon^2),\n    lambda*d_2-A_2\n      =epsilon[1+4/(lambda(1+epsilon))-lambda^2 epsilon]>0. (2)\n\nThe bracket is positive since lambda^2<6 and epsilon<=1/6144.\nAlso lambda*d_1-A_1=epsilon[-lambda+2/(lambda(1+epsilon))]<0.\nThus t_0,t_1<tstar<t_2<=t_j for j>=2. In particular b>1,d_2>0,\nand t_3>tstar. These strict signs are all that the negative test needs.\n\n## 3. Every inherited primitive and objective coefficient\n\nThe inherited array is rederived here. Its prior unverified reports are\nawareness, not premises. A primitive depth-j array Lambda^j has indices\n0,...,n_j. Start with Lambda^0=0 and zero diagonal. At a depth-(j+1)\nnode, put p=n_j, z=n_(j+1). Embed the left Lambda^j with factor1 and\nthe right Lambda^j with factor k_j. For each nonterminal right query i add\n\n    Lambda_(i,p) += u_j h_i, Lambda_(i,z) += v_j h_i,\n    Lambda_(z,p) += w_j,    Lambda_(p,z) += z_j,\n    u_j=2lambda,\n    v_j=[2lambda*d_j+2(A_j+d_j)]/A_j,\n    k_j=2+lambda+(lambda+1)t_j,\n    w_j=4A_j+2d_j-2lambda(A_j-1), z_j=2A_j.           (3)\n\nThe last two additions occur once per node, not once per right query.\nAll unspecified contributions are zero and overlapping ones are summed.\nEquivalently, for every dyadic node of depth l+1 and offset o=k*2^(l+1),\nwrite its indicated four local original-index row contributions from (3).\nMultiply each by the product of k_r over strict ancestors whose right child\ncontains that node, and sum over nodes. This is a finite sum/product formula\nfor EVERY Lambda^j entry, without an optimizer or a computed recurrence.\n\nAll signs are positive: w_j=2lambda+2(d_j-A_j/lambda)>2lambda for j>=2;\nw_0=4+2a>0; w_1>2lambda-2 because A_1<lambda and d_1>0.\nThe other factors in (3) are positive. The identities\n\n    u_j+v_j=2(k_j-1),\n    u_j(A_j-1)+w_j-z_j=2(A_j+d_j)\n\nshow that divergence is -2h_i at every primitive nonterminal and 2S_j\nat its terminal. For example a right nonterminal has divergence\n-2k_j h_i+(u_j+v_j)h_i=-2h_i. At p the divergence is\n2S_j+z_j-u_j S_j-w_j=-2(1+d_j). Total divergence is zero,\nso the terminal has the asserted remaining divergence.\n\nFor the objective array start Kbase^0=0, mu^0_0=2. At a join put\n\n    s_j=(S_j+1+d_j)/(3S_j+2(1+d_j)), theta_j=1-s_j,\n    Dc=D_j, Dp=D_(j+1), p=n_j, R_i=2^j+i.\n\nHere Kbase is the finite array and mu is the outgoing optimum profile.\nEvery coefficient of its next depth is\n\n    Kbase_(l,v)=Lambda^j_lv/Dp                    (left indices),\n    Kbase_(R_i,R_v)=Kbase^j_iv                   (right indices),\n    Kbase_(R_i,p)=s_j mu^j_i                    (including i=n_j),\n    mu_l=2h_l/Dp                               (0<=l<n_j),\n    mu_p=2s_j-2S_j/Dp,\n    mu_(R_i)=theta_j mu^j_i.                         (4)\n\nEvery incoming-optimum multiplier is zero in this CHOSEN base. This is not\na claimed normal form for general certificates. All unlisted finite entries\nare zero. One can unroll (4) without any ambiguity: for an objective depth-r\nword, its right-spine node of depth l has offset o_l=2^r-2^l. Insert its\nleft primitive array Lambda^(l-1)/D_l on o_l,...,o_l+n_(l-1); its bridge\nis o_l+n_(l-1); every right query sends s_(l-1) times the right-child mu\nto that bridge. A query's mu is its local 2h_i/D_l if a left nonterminal,\nor 2s_(l-1)-2S_(l-1)/D_l if that bridge, times\nproduct_(t=l)^(r-1)theta_t. The terminal has mu_n=2product_(t=0)^(r-1)theta_t.\nThe unique first such left-or-bridge category assigns each nonterminal.\nTogether with the finite node sum for Lambda, these specify every original\nKbase and mu coefficient by finitely many sums/products of physical scalars.\n\nThe bridge sign is positive: writing A=S_j,Bjoin=1+d_j, the numerator is\n(A+Bjoin)Dp-A(3A+2Bjoin)=A^2+4ABjoin+2Bjoin^2+A+Bjoin>0.\nAlso 0<s_j,theta_j<1. Primitive flow pays the left mu; at p the left\nterminal divergence plus mu_p equals 2s_j, canceled by its incoming\nright mass 2s_j. At every right query the old mu is split into its new\ntheta_j mu and its new s_j mu edge. Hence original value cancellation is\n\n    sum_v Kbase_iv-sum_v Kbase_vi+mu_i=2*1_(i=n),\n    sum_i mu_i=2, mu_i>0.                             (5)\n\nNo row points into the ACTUAL terminal n in this base. Internal child\nterminal indices are still present, including their incoming rows.\n\n## 4. Full polynomial identities for the base\n\nNormalize L=1 and x_star=g_star=f_star=0, as in fact1252b8823bb5a290.\nAll original vectors (x_0,g_0,...,g_n) remain independent. The column Ppos\nhas entries Ppos_i below. Define\n\n    x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t, Ppos_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, y_i=x_i-g_i,\n    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,\n    V_j=2sum_(i<n_j)h_i(f_i-f_(n_j))\n          +sum_(i<n_j)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2\n          -A_j(A_j-1)||g_(n_j)||^2,\n    Psrc_j=V_j+sum_(i!=v)Lambda^j_iv Q_iv.\n\nFlow cancels every value in Psrc. Let r_R=sum_(right nonterminal i)h_i g_i,\nY=r_R-(A_j-1)g_z, Z=g_p-g_z. Expansion of the four unweighted row sums\nin (3), in their listed order, gives the gradient expressions\n\n    d_j<r_R,g_p>+||r_R||^2/2+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2/2\n                                          +(A_j-1)||g_p||^2/2;\n    -||r_R||^2/2+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2/2\n                        +(A_j-1)||g_z||^2/2-<r_R,g_z>;\n    d_j<g_z,g_p>+<g_z,r_R>+(||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2)/2;\n    -(d_j+1/2)||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2/2-<g_p,r_R>-<g_p,g_z>.\n\nFor example the sum of forward within-right displacement terms is\n-||r_R||^2/2+sum h_i^2||g_i||^2/2; this fixes its diagonal correction.\nMultiply the displays by u_j,v_j,w_j,z_j and add V and the child terms.\nUsing lambda^2=2lambda+1 gives the complete identity\n\n    Psrc_(j+1)=Psrc_j^left+k_j Psrc_j^right\n      +(tstar-t_j)[(lambda+1)||Y||^2-2lambda A_j<Y,Z>\n                         +A_j^2(2+tstar+t_j)||Z||^2].    (6)\n\nThis is an identity on all original gradients. In particular it makes no\npositive-epsilon primitive PSD assertion.\n\nLet Cbase^j be the entire gradient matrix of the objective polynomial after\n(5). Put T_R=sum_i mu^j_i g_(R_i), x_p=x_0-sum_(l<n_j)h_l g_l and\n\n    Rchild=Cbase^j(g_R)-Dc||T_R||^2/4,\n    kappa_j=Dc-Dp theta_j^2.\n\nExpansion of all (4) rows gives\n\n    Mbase_(j+1)(1/Dp)=Psrc_j(g_left)/Dp+Rchild\n     +||x_p+(S_j-Dp s_j)g_p-(Dp theta_j/2)T_R||^2/Dp\n     +(kappa_j/4)||T_R-2g_p||^2.                       (7)\n\nFor a coefficient check, after subtracting the first two terms the remaining\npolynomial is exactly\n\n    ||x_p||^2/Dp+2(S_j/Dp-s_j)<x_p,g_p>\n    +(S_j^2/Dp+2s_j)||g_p||^2-theta_j<x_p,T_R>\n    +(1+d_j-s_j)<g_p,T_R>+Dc||T_R||^2/4.\n\nExpanding the two squares agrees because\ns_j(3S_j+2(1+d_j))=S_j+1+d_j. Neither Rchild nor kappa_j is assumed\nnonnegative for the deformed hierarchy.\n\nAt the full Huber contact tuple x_0=D_j, g_i=1, every square argument\nand each Y,Z in (6) is zero. Induction from Mbase_0=||x_0-g_0||^2\nalso makes the derivative of Rchild zero at its common-gradient vector.\nThus, writing e=(1,...,1),\n\n    Cbase e=D mu/2, e^T Cbase e=D.                     (8)\n\nFor the actual m>=1536 (indeed m>=2), one useful original entry is\nCbase_00=2/D, with mu_0=2b/D: only the\nfirst primitive depth1 term (2-b^2)||g_0-g_1||^2 contributes to the first\ngradient diagonal in the left source, and the x_p square contributes b^2/D.\nThese statements are jointly derived here, not imported from the last report.\n\n## 5. The two explicit additions and every final sign\n\nThe physical non-leaf checkpoints are the odd indices p<n. If level(p)=l,\nthen 1<=l<=m-1 and p=o+2^l-1 for a unique cell offset\no=k*2^(l+1), 0<=k<2^(m-l-1). Define the complete cell of original\ngradient indices as o,...,o+2^(l+1)-1, and its short-leaf set\n\n    E_(l,o)={o,o+2,...,o+2^(l+1)-2}.\n\nThese are actual even queried indices; every one has h_i=b. No terminal\ngradient is a member of a leaf set or checkpoint set.\n\nLAW A, preceding leaf. For each non-leaf p of level l put\n\n    omega_p=epsilon*A_l^2/D,\n    J^A_(p-1,p)=J^A_(p,p-1)=omega_p.                  (9)\n\nLAW B, whole-cell fan. For each (l,o) and p=o+2^l-1 put\n\n    gamma_(l,o)=epsilon*A_l^2/(D*2^l),\n    J^B_(i,p) += gamma_(l,o), J^B_(p,i) += gamma_(l,o)\n                         for every i in E_(l,o).      (10)\n\nAll other entries of J are zero and all stated contributions are summed.\nThe two laws are different supports, not two fits of the old optimum-row\ntransport. The factor 2^l makes their total added mass identical at every\nscale, allowing a direct comparison without silently increasing a budget.\nFor either law the FINAL original coefficients are\n\n    K_ij=Kbase_ij+J_ij (i!=j), K_ii=0,\n    lambda_(i,star)=mu_i, lambda_(star,i)=0,\n    q=21/(20D).                                      (11)\n\nAll final multipliers are nonnegative. Symmetry J_ij=J_ji preserves every\nvalue-flow equation (5). The optimum profile is fixed as a deliberate\nrestriction of these two laws; no claim is made that a general slack-spending\nconstruction may be reduced to this restriction. No decrement is hidden.\n\nEach law has the ONE entire-word multiplier-mass account\n\n    T79=(epsilon/D)sum_(l=1)^(m-1)2^(m-l-1)A_l^2,\n    sum_(i!=j)J_ij=2T79.                             (12)\n\nThis is not a sufficient PSD budget. The actual global objective/contact\nbudget, including all cross terms between cells, is given next.\n\n## 6. Every full entry, the changed contact, and its ONE Schur budget\n\nFor ANY array of the form (11) define out_i=mu_i+sum_v K_iv. The entire\nmatrix C in\n\n    M(q)=q||x_0||^2-sum_i mu_i<x_0,g_i>\n                       +sum_(i,j)C_ij<g_i,g_j>\n\nhas entries\n\n    C_ii=out_i-1_(i=n)-h_i sum_(v>i)K_iv,\n    2C_ij=-(K_ij+K_ji)-h_j sum_(v>j)K_iv\n                         +h_i[mu_j+sum_(v<=i)K_jv], i<j. (13)\n\nThe complete original matrix therefore has initial entry q and initial\nmixed entries -mu_i/2. To derive (13), flow says half the incident row mass\nequals out_i-1_(i=n). A forward row sourced at i contributes -h_i K_iv\nto its diagonal. For i<j the difference squares give -(K_ij+K_ji),\nrows out of i ending after j give the second term, and rows out of j ending\nat/before i or at the optimum give the last term. This also accounts for\nevery physical intermediate gradient in a nonlocal pair.\n\nFor an unordered pair represented by u<v let L_uv(g)=sum_(t=u)^(v-1)h_t g_t\nand ell_uv=Ppos_v-Ppos_u. The exact added polynomial per unit weight is\n\n    Q_uv+Q_vu=||g_u-g_v||^2-<g_u-g_v,L_uv(g)>\n             =<g_u-g_v,y_v-y_u>.                     (14)\n\nFor v=u+1 across b this is\n(1-b)||g_u||^2+(b-2)<g_u,g_v>+||g_v||^2. Its determinant is -b^2/4,\nso it is indefinite whenever b>0. It is not a free PSD correction.\n\nAt g=e the value of (14) is zero, but its gradient is\nell_uv(e_v-e_u). Consequently define the entire contact-defect column\n\n    d79=(1/2)sum_(unordered {u,v},u<v)J_uv*ell_uv*(e_v-e_u).\n\nHere e_i denotes the standard coordinate column, distinct from e. Then\n\n    Ce=D mu/2+d79, e^T d79=0, e^T C e=D.              (15)\n\nFor A the formula is d79=(b/2)sum_p omega_p(e_p-e_(p-1)). For B it is\n(1/2)sum_(l,o)gamma_(l,o)sum_(i in E_(l,o))(Ppos_p-Ppos_i)(e_p-e_i),\nwith signed Ppos_p-Ppos_i valid on both sides of the checkpoint. Each is one\nfinite original-index vector, not separate contact credits. Both are nonzero:\n\n    Ppos^T d79=(1/2)sum_(u<v)J_uv(Ppos_v-Ppos_u)^2>0.           (16)\n\nAll physical steps are positive and each law has a positive added edge.\nThus Ce=D mu/2 is NOT assumed; it is false for both candidates.\n\nAt x_0=D,g=e the complete value is (qD-1)D=D/20. Its initial/common\ntwo-by-two block is\n\n    Bcommon=[[q,-1],[-1,D]], det Bcommon=qD-1=1/20.\n\nThis is the ONE scalar allowance for the whole word. Write arbitrary\ngradients as g=t e+v with e^T v=0, first in one real coordinate and then\ncoordinatewise in a Euclidean space. Set u=mu^T v and z=d79^T v. Their\nmixed column with (x_0,t) is (-u/2,Du/2+z). Exact inversion gives\n\n    (-u/2,Du/2+z) Bcommon^(-1) (-u/2,Du/2+z)^T\n       =D u^2/4+u z+[q/(qD-1)]z^2\n       =D u^2/4+u z+21z^2/D.                        (17)\n\nFor clarity the numerator before dividing by qD-1 is\nD(qD-1)u^2/4+(qD-1)uz+qz^2. Thus all contact, common-gradient and\ninitial costs have been charged exactly once. In particular the last term\nis the square of the WHOLE sum d79^T v and includes all cross-cell terms.\nIt cannot be paid anew from 1/20 at every node.\n\nEquivalently, the entire remaining gradient matrix is\n\n    R79=C-D mu mu^T/4-(mu d79^T+d79 mu^T)/2\n                                      -(21/D)d79 d79^T,\n    R79 e=0.                                        (18)\n\nFull PSD at q=21/(20D) is equivalent to R79 being PSD on the entire\ne-perpendicular space, or equivalently on all gradient coordinates since\nR79 e=0. Equations (17)--(18) are a necessary-and-sufficient expenditure\nidentity, NOT a claim that either law pays it successfully. The direct\nnegative full-polynomial test below disproves that claim.\n\n## 7. Actual terminal-square expenditure\n\nNeither added support touches n. The final incoming-terminal coefficients\nremain zero by the explicit formulas, not by deleting rows from a supplied\ncertificate. Full terminal flow is therefore sum_j lambda_(n,j)=2 and\nC_nn=1. More explicitly the receivers are star and the right-spine bridges\n\n    p_l=2^m-2^l-1, 0<=l<m,\n    alpha_star=product_(v=0)^(m-1)theta_v,\n    alpha_(p_l)=s_l product_(v=0)^(l-1)theta_v.        (19)\n\nAn empty product is1. All other alpha_i are zero. The finite telescoping\nidentity sum_l s_l product_(v<l)theta_v=1-product_(v<m)theta_v shows these\nare probabilities. They are exactly lambda_(n,j)/2 from (4), including\nthe adjacent final bridge p_0=n-1. No unqueried gradient is discarded.\n\nThe exact vector in the terminal square is\n\n    Vterm=alpha_star x_0\n         -sum_(t<n)[h_t w_t-(w_t-w_(t-1))]g_t,\n    w_(-1)=alpha_star, w_t=alpha_star+sum_(i=0)^t alpha_i.\n\nThe complete remainder is\n\n    M(q)=||g_n-Vterm||^2+Rprefix,\n    Rprefix=q||x_0||^2-2sum_(j in {star,0,...,n-1})alpha_j\n                    (f_j-||g_j||^2/2)-||Vterm||^2\n                  +sum_(i!=j in {star,0,...,n-1})lambda_ij Q_ij. (20)\n\nThe prefix flow cancels every f_j. Formula (20) follows by expanding the\nterminal rows, as in\nfact1252b8823bb5a290(c), and retains the actual negative square cost.\nEquivalently put vterm=(mu_n/2)x_0-sum_(i<n)C_in g_i; then Vterm=vterm\nand Rprefix=q||x_0||^2-sum_(i<n)mu_i<x_0,g_i>\n+sum_(i,j<n)C_ij<g_i,g_j>-||vterm||^2.\nSince the added rows contain no terminal gradients, they do not alter\nC_in or vterm. They add their FULL (14) expressions to Rprefix.\n\n## 8. Original-space block direction and its complete base action\n\nPut\n\n    W79=A_2+d_2, Bfirst=b+1+d_1,\n    Edef=(t_2-tstar)A_2^2(lambda+t_2)>0,\n    delta3=t_3-tstar>0.\n\nW79 is the physical mass of steps0,...,3 and of steps8,...,11.\nThe notation is local and does not replace W_n(h). In the ENTIRE original\nformal space assign scalar values\n\n    x_0=0,\n    g_i=1 for i=0,1,2,3;\n    g_i=-1 for i=8,9,10,11;\n    g_i=0 for every other i=0,...,n.                 (21)\n\nThe two active four-step blocks have identical physical words. Hence the\ntotal displacement is W79-W79=0. All positions from index12 onward are0;\ng_7=g_15=g_n=0. These are chosen test values, not equality constraints\non formal coordinates and not a legal primal trajectory.\n\nIn the first H3, its left H2 gradients are all1, its right H2 all0,\nso both child Psrc terms vanish by (6), and Y=0,Z=1 gives -Edef.\nThe H3 beginning at8 gives the same -Edef. At their H4 join,\nY=-W79,Z=0. Thus the COMPLETE source polynomial is\n\n    Psrc_4=-(1+k_3)Edef-delta3(lambda+1)W79^2<0.     (22)\n\nFor every containing source ancestor all its right gradients, bridge and\nterminal are zero, so Y=Z=0 and (6) propagates exactly (22), with left\nfactor1. At the top objective join the left displacement, x_p, g_p and\nT_R are allzero; Rchild on its zero right gradients is zero. Equation (7)\ntherefore gives for every m>=5, in particular all m>=1536,\n\n    Mbase(q;21)=Psrc_4/D<0 for EVERY real q.           (23)\n\nNo positive-epsilon primitive child or quotient feasibility has been used.\nThe parameter signs came from the exact SAME-m (2).\n\n## 9. Law A: every preceding-leaf symmetric pair vanishes\n\nEvery edge in (9) connects an even index to the next odd index. Assignment\n(21) is constant on every such pair, including pairs outside its support.\nThus g_(p-1)-g_p=0 and its ENTIRE (14) polynomial is zero. All preceding\ngradient costs and cross terms cancel exactly; no diagonal-only inference\nis involved. Summing every added pair gives\n\n    M_A(q;21)=Mbase(q;21)=Psrc_4/D<0.                 (24)\n\nThis proves a stronger, precise scope: for the fixed inherited base, ANY\nnonnegative weights omega_p on this support fail for every q. Allowing\ndifferent scale laws, horizon dependence, arbitrary magnitudes or zero\nweights cannot change (24). The new support is not a repair of the earlier\nH4 defect. This is a formal PSD obstruction, not an interpolable witness.\n\n## 10. Law B: every full-cell fan has nonpositive action\n\nFor a cell define its UNWEIGHTED full polynomial\n\n    F_(l,o)=sum_(i in E_(l,o))<g_i-g_p,y_p-y_i>, p=o+2^l-1.\n\nThen M_B-Mbase=sum_(l,o)gamma_(l,o)F_(l,o), with no omitted rows.\nWe evaluate every nonzero cell on (21), including cells whose checkpoint\nis zero but whose source leaves are nonzero.\n\nAt l=1 each cell comprises four consecutive gradient indices; (21) is\nconstant on that cell. Its fan is zero. At l=2 only the cells o=0 and\no=8 can contribute. For o=0, p=3 has gradient1. The left leaves0,2 have\nthe same gradient and contribute zero. The right leaves4,6 have gradient0,\nposition -W79 and y=-W79, whereas y_3=-A_2. Each contributes\n(-1)(W79-A_2)=-d_2. At o=8 the entire geometry through15 is the negative\nof the first cell, apart from a common positional translation. A common\ntranslation cancels in y_p-y_i, and negating both factors preserves their\nproduct. Its two nonzero terms likewise equal -d_2. Therefore\n\n    F_(2,0)=F_(2,8)=-2d_2<0.                        (25)\n\nAt l=3 only the first cell is active, with p=7, g_p=0 and y_p=-W79.\nIts nonzero source leaves are0,2,8,10. Their original proximal positions\nare respectively -1,-Bfirst-1,-W79+1,-W79+Bfirst+1.\nThe four complete terms, in this order, are\n\n    1-W79, 1-W79+Bfirst, 1, 1+Bfirst.\n\nTheir sum is 4-2W79+2Bfirst. Since\nW79=2b+2+d_1+d_2 and Bfirst=b+1+d_1, this is\n\n    F_(3,0)=-2(b+d_2-1)<0.                          (26)\n\nThe positive terms1 and1+Bfirst have been explicitly paid by the negative\nterms; they are not omitted. At EVERY l>=4 only the first cell can have\nnonzero endpoint gradients, and its checkpoint has y_p=g_p=0. The same\nfour source leaves now contribute\n\n    1, 1+Bfirst, 1-W79, 1-W79+Bfirst,\n\nwhose sum is again -2(b+d_2-1). Every other source and checkpoint has\nzero gradient, so its factor g_i-g_p is zero, even if a position is nonzero.\nAll cells not listed are either disjoint from the support or were covered\nby the constant-cell calculation. {"id": "20c7af333e2c7cbf", "timestamp_utc": "2026-10-02T16:14:27.034790+00:00", "author": "max4", "kind": "proof_attempt", "claim": "Independent max4 audit found no gap at the complete frozen benchmark scope: all-split C_n and B_n envelopes, same-enclosing-m ratio 47/50, and ONLY conditional 799/800 implication. Package is UNVERIFIED pending the sole allocated gate; original Goal remains OPEN.", "evidence": "# Independent audit of the frozen benchmark package\n\nAuditor: max4, invocation 77. Status: UNVERIFIED independent mathematical review.\nVerdict: no_gap_found_at_declared_scope.\nThis verdict is not verifier acceptance and is not a resolution of the original minimax problem.\n\nFrozen source: DRAFT_UNVERIFIED.md in this directory.\nSHA256: 34ea5110540b43c7598efb96dc5692a5ce93ab5594199bf9af0052f4be65cf4a.\nThe source was read in full and has not been edited.\n\n## Scope and dependency audit\n\nI checked all three assertions together: both all-n/all-split power envelopes;\nthe same-enclosing-m hierarchy ratio for every integer m>=1536; and the\nconditional 799/800 implication. The full-function upper 17/(16D_m) is NOT\nproved and is NOT an input fact. The statement's last assertion is an implication\nwith precisely that hypothesis.\n\nThe permitted accepted fact b98bb807d514e80d was read in full. It identifies the\noriginal mass recurrences and W normalization. None of its coarse estimates is\nneeded. In particular, neither 04219554e41d9750's saved endpoints nor the earlier\nunverified envelope 48010339bbfbce4f is a proof premise. Fact search produced\nno duplicate of this complete package. Broad initial memory queries were\ntruncated; the full controlling guidance f6b7cc9ddb9c8d02 and needed inputs were\nthen obtained in bounded reads. An rg search for local skill files returned no\nmatches (exit 1); it was not a mathematical failure.\n\nA Matlas query about the primitive power inequality returned two unrelated\ntheorems (1609.04774 Theorem 2.5 and 1703.09411 Lemma 3.4). Neither is used.\nA web terminology search distinguished convexity in log coordinates from\nordinary log-convexity; no source theorem enters the proof. The full calculation\nis provided below and in PROOF.md. No external_refs entry is needed.\n\n## Independent attack: normalization and final implication first\n\nFor every finite nonnegative word h, W_n(h) is the supremum of twice the\nobjective gap divided by L times the squared distance to the chosen minimizer.\nRemoving that numerator factor defines R_n(h), so W_n(h)=2R_n(h).\nW_n_star is the infimum over all such words, and therefore\nW_n_star<=W_n(H_m) at n=2^m-1. Multiplication is by B_n>0, since U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.\nThus an upper estimate for B_n, combined with a lower estimate for D_m, gives\nthe required UPPER ratio B_n/D_m. The direction is correct.\n\nUnder the stated unproved hypothesis, the resulting ratio is strictly below\n(17/16)(47/50)=799/800 for every integer m>=1536. Since 2^m-1 is an unbounded\nsequence of actual integer horizons, the liminf over all horizons is at most\n799/800. This requires no existence of a global limit and no supremum exchange.\nThere is no unconditional conclusion about W_n(H_m).\n\n## Independent attack: same-m mass and endpoint m=1536\n\nEvery bridge 1+a*c^j is positive: epsilon=1/(4m) lies in (0,1/6144],\nbeta=1-epsilon>0, c=lambda(1+epsilon)>lambda>2, and a=beta/c>0.\nConcatenation gives length(H_j)=2^j-1, S_0=0, and\nS_(j+1)=2S_j+1+a*c^j. Hence A_j=1+S_j satisfies\nA_(j+1)=2A_j+a*c^j, A_0=1.\n\nLet v=a/(c-2). Substituting (1-v)2^j+v*c^j into the recurrence gives\n2(1-v)2^j+(2v+a)c^j, which is the proposed next term because\n2v+a=cv. The initial value is 1. Since lambda(lambda-2)=1 and c>lambda,\nc(c-2)>1; consequently 0<v<1. This proves A_m>=v*c^m without changing m at\nany child.\n\nThe denominator expansion is\nc(c-2)=1+2(lambda+1)epsilon+lambda^2 epsilon^2.\nThe bounds lambda<5/2 and lambda^2=2lambda+1<6 give\nv>(1-epsilon)/(1+7epsilon+6epsilon^2).\nThe comparison to 1-8epsilon follows by multiplying the positive denominator:\n(1-8epsilon)(1+7epsilon+6epsilon^2)\n=1-epsilon-50epsilon^2-48epsilon^3<1-epsilon.\nAll factors used when multiplying lower bounds are positive for m>=1536.\n\nKeeping only the first three nonnegative binomial terms gives\n(1+1/(4m))^m>=41/32-1/(32m). Also 2^m>m for every integer m>=1\n(by induction), hence lambda^(-m)<1/m. Expansion of the resulting product is\n2(1-2/m)(41/32-1/(32m))-1/m\n=41/16-99/(16m)+1/(8m^2).\nDiscarding the last positive term gives a strict lower bound. At m=1536,\n41/16-99/24576=(62976-99)/24576=20959/8192.\nThe cross products are 20959*47=985073 and 8192*120=983040.\nTheir difference is 2033>0. The endpoint is included; no eventual unspecified\nthreshold was substituted. Together with B_(2^m-1)<=(12/5)lambda^m this proves\nthe strict ratio 47/50 with the correct direction.\n\n## Independent attack: primitive envelope\n\nFirst lambda^3=7+5sqrt(2)>14 because (7/5)^2<2.\nThus lambda^15>14^5=537824>524288=2^19, and rho>19/15>1.\n\nFor t>0 set E(t)=(1+t)^rho-1>0 and Q(z)=log E(exp z).\nWriting t=exp z gives\nQ'(z)=rho*t*(1+t)^(rho-1)/E(t).\nDifferentiating once more and collecting the numerator gives\nQ''(z)=rho*t*(1+t)^(rho-2)\n *[(1+rho*t)E(t)-rho*t*(1+t)^rho]/E(t)^2\n=rho*t*(1+t)^(rho-2)\n *[(1+t)^rho-1-rho*t]/E(t)^2.\nThe bracket has value zero at t=0 and strictly positive derivative for t>0,\nso Q is convex. Its midpoint inequality at z and -z yields\nE(t)E(1/t)>=(2^rho-1)^2=2.\n\nFor positive x,y and T=(x^(1/rho)+y^(1/rho))^rho, multiplication gives\n(T-x)(T-y)>=2xy. Equivalently T^2-(x+y)T-xy>=0.\nIts negative root is strictly negative and T>0, so T>=Omega(x,y).\nIf x=0 or y=0, the asserted inequality is equality directly; at x=y=0 both\nsides vanish. No use of log 0 or division by 0 is needed.\nOmega is coordinatewise nondecreasing on the closed nonnegative quadrant\nbecause both its linear part and the polynomial under its square root are.\nInduction uses k+1 and n-k, both positive for every allowed integer split,\nwhose sum is n+1. The n=0 case is equality.\n\n## Independent attack: sharper scalar inequality and global maximum\n\nIt suffices to use p=19/30 in (5/6)x^(2p)+(1-x)^p<=1 because the bases are\nin [0,1] and p>0. Endpoint powers are well-defined including base zero.\nLet Psi(x)=(5/6)x^(19/15)+(1-x)^(19/30).\nIts derivative on (0,1) is\n(19/18)x^(4/15)-(19/30)(1-x)^(-11/30).\nA critical point therefore satisfies\nx^(4/15)(1-x)^(11/30)=3/5,\nequivalently x^8(1-x)^11=(3/5)^30.\n\nThe logarithmic derivative of x^8(1-x)^11 is\n(8-19x)/(x(1-x)); it is negative on (8/19,1).\nFor x>=2/3 the left side is at most 2^8/3^19. The strict comparison to the\ncritical value follows from the exact integer identities\n2^8*5^30=238418579101562500000000\n<239299329230617529590083=3^49.\nThese are integer products, not a floating point approximation. The powers\ncan be checked by successive integer multiplication, for example from\n5^10=9765625, 3^10=59049 and 3^9=19683. No program was run for them.\nThus every critical point lies strictly below 2/3.\n\nSubstituting the derivative relation gives the exact critical value\ng(x)=(1-x/2)/(1-x)^(11/30).\nIts logarithmic derivative is\n-1/(2-x)+11/[30(1-x)]\n=(19x-8)/[30(1-x)(2-x)].\nIt decreases then increases on [0,2/3], so its maximum there is at an endpoint.\ng(0)=1 and g(2/3)^30=2^30/3^19<1, since\n2^30=1073741824<1162261467=3^19.\nPsi is continuous on [0,1], differentiable in its interior, with endpoint\nvalues 1 and 5/6. Even though the derivative is singular at x=1, an interior\nmaximum must be critical and endpoints are checked separately. This exhausts\nthe possible maximizers without assuming how many critical points exist.\n\n## Independent attack: dominant induction\n\nFor x,y>=0,\nsqrt(G(x,y))=(sqrt(y)+sqrt(y+8x))/2.\nSquaring proves this identity. The expression is the larger nonnegative root\nof z^2-sqrt(y)z-2x=0, and is positive unless both x and y vanish.\nFor a positive trial z, nonnegativity of this polynomial puts z above that\nroot; the other root is nonpositive. G is coordinatewise nondecreasing.\n\nSet kappa=12/5. At every split, A=k+1>=1, F=n-k>=1, N=A+F=n+1.\nThe trial z=sqrt(kappa)N^(rho/2) has nonnegative root polynomial exactly when\n(2/kappa)(A/N)^rho+(F/N)^(rho/2)<=1.\nThis is the scalar inequality with p=rho/2>19/30. Strong induction on B_n,\nusing the already established C bound, controls each split and hence its\nfull maximum. B_0=1<=12/5 is included.\n\n## Editorial changes authorized for the new package\n\nThe new statement expands W_n_star and R_n and the full objective class.\nThe proof uses E_be,Q_be,Psi_be,g_be,A_be_j,v_be,kappa_be for local auxiliaries\nto avoid clashes with unrelated project glossary entries. H_j, S_j, a and c\nare explicitly local hierarchy notation. Positive-root endpoint language is\nmade precise: a root at (0,0) is nonnegative, not strictly positive.\nThe derivative calculations, signs, finite-horizon n=0 case, positivity of\nevery multiplying factor, and subsequence liminf argument are expanded.\nThese are editorial expansions, not changed constants, quantifiers, hypotheses,\nor conclusions. No weakened statement is proposed.\n\nNo mathematical program, numerical/arithmetic replay, trajectory or recurrence\nexecution, optimizer, solver, benchmark DP, installation, paid consult, or\nsubagent was used. Administrative reading, hashing, JSON serialization and\nreceipt handling are separate from the mathematical reasoning.\n", "verifiable": true, "status": "unverified", "fact_id": null, "links": {"source_sha256": "34ea5110540b43c7598efb96dc5692a5ce93ab5594199bf9af0052f4be65cf4a", "audit_path": "/opt/danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/benchmark_envelope_20261003/AUDIT_UNVERIFIED.md", "accepted_definitions": "b98bb807d514e80d", "guidance": "f6b7cc9ddb9c8d02"}, "glossary": {"E_be": "Auxiliary positive function (1+t)^rho-1, called E locally in the audit.", "Q_be": "Auxiliary function log(E_be(exp z)), called Q locally in the audit."}}
{"id": "e9278cb7e2f8f4f9", "timestamp_utc": "2026-10-02T17:04:48.088759+00:00", "author": "max2", "kind": "proof_attempt", "claim": "JOINT UNVERIFIED max2r70: explicit genuine 2D rotating three-group centered-simplex inverse law, all original rows/thresholds and OWN radius.
